Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any plasma_physics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 722. The projective resolution has length at most 337 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_722(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 722. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^337(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in plasma_physics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 214 and the invariant I(A,B) = 575 mod 4.
